We evaluate our approach in text-based embodied environments such as ALFWorld and MiniGrid and show that deferring only about 15% of decisions to the large model can match the quality of using it exclusively, while significantly reducing inference costs.

</details>


### [77] [STRIDE-ED: A Strategy-Grounded Stepwise Reasoning Framework for Empathetic Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07100)
*Hongru Ji,Yuyin Fan,Meng Zhao,Xianghua Li,Lianwei Wu,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STRIDE-ED：一个基于策略、可解释的深度推理框架，通过结构化、策略条件化的推理来建模共情对话，解决了现有方法在策略框架、多阶段推理和高质量数据方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共情对话方法存在三个根本性限制：缺乏全面的共情策略框架、缺乏明确的任务对齐多阶段推理、缺乏高质量的策略感知数据，这阻碍了将共情对话有效建模为复杂的多阶段认知和决策过程。

Method: 1. 提出STRIDE-ED框架，通过结构化、策略条件化的推理建模共情对话；2. 开发策略感知数据精炼流程，集成基于LLM的标注、多模型一致性加权评估和动态采样；3. 采用两阶段训练范式，结合监督微调与多目标强化学习。

Result: 大量实验表明，STRIDE-ED能够泛化到多种开源LLM，并在自动指标和人工评估上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: STRIDE-ED通过结构化策略条件推理、高质量数据构建和两阶段训练，有效解决了共情对话建模中的关键挑战，实现了更优的性能表现。

Abstract: Empathetic dialogue requires not only recognizing a user's emotional state but also making strategy-aware, context-sensitive decisions throughout response generation. However, the lack of a comprehensive empathy strategy framework, explicit task-aligned multi-stage reasoning, and high-quality strategy-aware data fundamentally limits existing approaches, preventing them from effectively modeling empathetic dialogue as a complex, multi-stage cognitive and decision-making process. To address these challenges, we propose STRIDE-ED, a STRategy-grounded, Interpretable, and DEep reasoning framework that models Empathetic Dialogue through structured, strategy-conditioned reasoning. To support effective learning, we develop a strategy-aware data refinement pipeline integrating LLM-based annotation, multi-model consistency-weighted evaluation, and dynamic sampling to construct high-quality training data aligned with empathetic strategies. Furthermore, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm that combines supervised fine-tuning with multi-objective reinforcement learning to better align model behaviors with target emotions, empathetic strategies, and response formats. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STRIDE-ED generalizes across diverse open-source LLMs and consistently outperforms existing methods on both automatic metrics and human evaluations.

</details>


### [78] [IndoBERT-Sentiment: Context-Conditioned Sentiment Classification for Indonesian Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07057)
*Muhammad Apriandito Arya Saputra,Andry Alamsyah,Dian Puteri Ramadhani,Thomhert Suprapto Siadari,Hanif Fakhrurroja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IndoBERT-Sentiment：一种基于主题上下文的情感分类器，通过结合主题背景和文本输入，显著提升印尼语情感分析性能，相比无上下文方法提升35.6 F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有印尼语情感分析模型孤立地分类文本，忽略了决定语句情感极性（积极、消极或中性）的主题上下文。需要开发能够理解文本在特定主题背景下情感含义的模型。

Method: 基于IndoBERT Large（3.35亿参数）构建上下文条件化情感分类器，输入包括主题上下文和文本。在188个主题的31,360个上下文-文本对上进行训练，使模型能够基于讨论主题进行情感预测。

Result: 模型在测试集上达到F1宏平均0.856和准确率88.1%。与三种广泛使用的通用印尼语情感模型相比，IndoBERT-Sentiment比最佳基线高出35.6 F1分数。模型能够正确分类无上下文方法系统误判的文本。

Conclusion: 上下文条件化方法（先前在相关性分类中证明有效）成功迁移到情感分析任务，显著提升印尼语情感分类性能。该方法使模型能够基于主题背景正确理解文本情感，解决了无上下文方法的系统性问题。

Abstract: Existing Indonesian sentiment analysis models classify text in isolation, ignoring the topical context that often determines whether a statement is positive, negative, or neutral. We introduce IndoBERT-Sentiment, a context-conditioned sentiment classifier that takes both a topical context and a text as input, producing sentiment predictions grounded in the topic being discussed. Built on IndoBERT Large (335M parameters) and trained on 31,360 context-text pairs labeled across 188 topics, the model achieves an F1 macro of 0.856 and accuracy of 88.1%. In a head-to-head evaluation against three widely used general-purpose Indonesian sentiment models on the same test set, IndoBERT-Sentiment outperforms the best baseline by 35.6 F1 points. We show that context-conditioning, previously demonstrated for relevancy classification, transfers effectively to sentiment analysis and enables the model to correctly classify texts that are systematically misclassified by context-free approaches.

</details>


### [79] [The Impact of Steering Large Language Models with Persona Vectors in Educational Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07102)
*Yongchao Wu,Aron Henriksson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探索了激活引导的人格向量在教育场景中对大语言模型生成和评分的影响，发现人格引导会降低答案质量，且对开放式人文学科任务的影响远大于事实性科学任务；评分方面则出现可预测的人格对齐校准偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于激活的引导可以在推理时个性化大语言模型，但其在教育场景中的效果尚不明确。本研究旨在系统性地探索人格引导在教育文本生成和自动评分中的影响，为教育环境中部署引导模型提供实证依据。

Method: 在ASAP-SAS基准测试上，为七种人格特征创建人格向量，应用于三种涵盖两种架构的模型。研究比较了短答案生成和自动评分任务，特别关注开放式英语语言艺术（ELA）提示与事实性科学提示的差异，以及解释性和论证性任务的敏感性。

Result: 人格引导总体上降低了答案质量，对开放式ELA提示的影响远大于事实性科学提示，解释性和论证性任务的敏感性高达11倍。评分方面观察到可预测的人格对齐校准偏移：邪恶和不礼貌的评分者评分更严苛，而善良和乐观的评分者评分更宽松。ELA任务对评分者个性化的敏感性是科学任务的2.5-3倍，混合专家模型的校准偏移大约是密集模型的6倍。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统研究激活引导人格特征在教育生成和评分中影响的研究。结果表明，在教育环境中部署引导模型时，需要考虑任务感知和架构感知的校准，因为不同任务类型和模型架构对人格引导的敏感性存在显著差异。

Abstract: Activation-based steering can personalize large language models at inference time, but its effects in educational settings remain unclear. We study persona vectors for seven character traits in short-answer generation and automated scoring on the ASAP-SAS benchmark across three models spanning two architectures. Persona steering lowers answer quality overall, with much larger effects on open-ended English Language Arts (ELA) prompts than on factual science prompts; interpretive and argumentative tasks are up to 11x more sensitive. On the scoring side, we observe predictable valence-aligned calibration shifts: evil and impolite scorers grade more harshly, while good and optimistic scorers grade more leniently. ELA tasks are 2.5-3x more susceptible to scorer personalization than science tasks, and the Mixture-of-Experts model shows roughly 6x larger calibration shifts than the dense models. To our knowledge, this is the first study to systematically examine the effects of activation-steered persona traits in educational generation and scoring, and the results highlight the need for task-aware and architecture-aware calibration when deploying steered models in educational settings.

</details>


### [80] [SemEval-2026 Task 3: Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07066)
*Liang-Chih Yu,Jonas Becker,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Idris Abdulmumin,Lung-Hao Lee,Ying-Lung Lin,Jin Wang,Jan Philip Wahle,Terry Ruas,Natalia Loukachevitch,Alexander Panchenko,Ilseyar Alimova,Lilian Wanzare,Nelson Odhiambo,Bela Gipp,Kai-Wei Chang,Saif M. Mohammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SemEval-2026共享任务提出维度方面情感分析(DimABSA)和维度立场分析(DimStance)，将传统ABSA从分类标签扩展到价-唤醒度(VA)维度建模，并引入连续F1(cF1)指标评估结构化提取和VA回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方面情感分析(ABSA)使用分类极性标签存在局限性，需要扩展到价-唤醒度(VA)维度建模以捕捉更丰富的情感信息。同时需要将ABSA从消费者评论扩展到公共议题讨论(政治、能源、气候等)，通过将立场目标视为方面，在VA空间中重新构建立场检测任务。

Method: 任务分为两个轨道：Track A (DimABSA)包含三个子任务：(1)维度方面情感回归，(2)维度方面情感三元组提取，(3)维度方面情感四元组提取；Track B (DimStance)仅包含立场目标的回归子任务。引入连续F1(cF1)指标联合评估结构化提取和VA回归性能。

Result: 任务吸引了超过400名参与者，收到112份最终提交和42篇系统描述论文。报告了基线结果，分析了表现最佳的系统，并讨论了关键设计选择，为方面和立场目标层面的维度情感分析提供了见解。

Conclusion: 该共享任务成功推动了维度情感分析的发展，通过VA维度建模和连续评估指标，为ABSA和立场分析提供了更精细的情感表示框架。所有资源已在GitHub仓库中公开，促进了该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: We present the SemEval-2026 shared task on Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA), which improves traditional ABSA by modeling sentiment along valence-arousal (VA) dimensions rather than using categorical polarity labels. To extend ABSA beyond consumer reviews to public-issue discourse (e.g., political, energy, and climate issues), we introduce an additional task, Dimensional Stance Analysis (DimStance), which treats stance targets as aspects and reformulates stance detection as regression in the VA space. The task consists of two tracks: Track A (DimABSA) and Track B (DimStance). Track A includes three subtasks: (1) dimensional aspect sentiment regression, (2) dimensional aspect sentiment triplet extraction, and (3) dimensional aspect sentiment quadruplet extraction, while Track B includes only the regression subtask for stance targets. We also introduce a continuous F1 (cF1) metric to jointly evaluate structured extraction and VA regression. The task attracted more than 400 participants, resulting in 112 final submissions and 42 system description papers. We report baseline results, discuss top-performing systems, and analyze key design choices to provide insights into dimensional sentiment analysis at the aspect and stance-target levels. All resources are available on our GitHub repository.

</details>


### [81] [Dynamic Context Evolution for Scalable Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07147)
*Ryan Lingo,Rajeev Chhajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出动态上下文演化（DCE）框架，通过三种机制解决大语言模型跨批次模式崩溃问题，显著提升输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在独立多次提示时会产生重复输出（跨批次模式崩溃），现有方法依赖临时去重和种子轮换，缺乏系统化解决方案。

Method: 提出DCE框架：1）言语化尾部采样（模型自我评估并丢弃明显想法）；2）语义记忆（持久嵌入索引拒绝跨批次近重复）；3）自适应提示演化（基于记忆状态和多样性策略重构提示）。

Result: 在三个领域（可持续包装概念、教育考试题目、创意写作提示）和两个模型系列（GPT-5-mini和Claude-haiku-4-5）的实验中，DCE实现0.0%崩溃率（vs 朴素提示的5.6%），每种子产生17-18个聚类（vs 2-17个），概念结构更丰富。

Conclusion: DCE有效解决跨批次模式崩溃问题，去重和提示演化单独使用不足但联合有效，成本约0.50美元/千候选，无需微调或定制架构。

Abstract: Large language models produce repetitive output when prompted independently across many batches, a phenomenon we term cross-batch mode collapse: the progressive loss of output diversity when a language model is prompted repeatedly without access to its prior generations. Practitioners have long mitigated this with ad hoc deduplication and seed rotation, but no principled framework exists. We introduce Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE), comprising three mechanisms: (1) verbalized tail sampling (the model labels each idea with a guess about how obvious it is, and obvious ideas are discarded), which filters high-probability candidates via model self-assessment; (2) semantic memory, which maintains a persistent embedding index to reject near-duplicates across batches; and (3) adaptive prompt evolution, which reconstructs the generation prompt each batch using memory state and rotating diversity strategies. In experiments across three domains (sustainable packaging concepts, educational exam questions, and creative writing prompts) and two model families (gpt-5-mini and claude-haiku-4-5), a component ablation across 2-3 random seeds per method shows that DCE achieves 0.0 +/- 0.0% collapse versus 5.6 +/- 2.0% for naive prompting, while producing 17-18 HDBSCAN clusters per seed versus naive's volatile 2-17, indicating reliably richer conceptual structure. These results are validated with an independent embedding model (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) and hold across sensitivity sweeps of the VTS threshold tau and dedup threshold delta. Deduplication and prompt evolution are individually insufficient but jointly effective, at approximately $0.50 per 1,000 candidates using only standard API calls, with no fine-tuning or custom architectures required.

</details>


### [82] [Is Cross-Lingual Transfer in Bilingual Models Human-Like? A Study with Overlapping Word Forms in Dutch and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07067)
*Iza Škrjanec,Irene Elisabeth Winther,Vera Demberg,Stefan L. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨双语语言模型是否重现人类双语阅读中的跨语言激活模式，发现模型仅在特定词汇共享条件下才能复现人类处理模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双语者在阅读时会出现跨语言激活，同源词（cognates）通常产生促进效应，而跨语言同形异义词（interlingual homographs）则导致干扰或无效应。本研究旨在探究双语语言模型中的跨语言激活是否与这些人类处理模式相似。

Method: 训练荷兰语-英语因果Transformer模型，采用四种词汇共享条件来操纵（假）同源词是否共享或具有语言特定的嵌入。使用双语阅读研究中的心理语言学刺激材料，通过惊奇度（surprisal）和嵌入相似性分析评估模型。

Result: 模型在很大程度上保持语言分离，跨语言效应主要在嵌入共享时出现。在这些情况下，同源词和跨语言同形异义词相对于控制词都显示促进效应。回归分析显示这些效应主要由频率驱动，而非形式-意义映射的一致性。只有当同源词共享嵌入时，才能复现双语者的定性模式。

Conclusion: 双语语言模型能够捕捉一些跨语言激活效应，但其与人类处理的对应关系似乎关键取决于词汇重叠如何编码，这可能限制了它们作为双语阅读模型的解释充分性。

Abstract: Bilingual speakers show cross-lingual activation during reading, especially for words with shared surface form. Cognates (friends) typically lead to facilitation, whereas interlingual homographs (false friends) cause interference or no effect. We examine whether cross-lingual activation in bilingual language models mirrors these patterns. We train Dutch-English causal Transformers under four vocabulary-sharing conditions that manipulate whether (false) friends receive shared or language-specific embeddings. Using psycholinguistic stimuli from bilingual reading studies, we evaluate the models through surprisal and embedding similarity analyses. The models largely maintain language separation, and cross-lingual effects arise primarily when embeddings are shared. In these cases, both friends and false friends show facilitation relative to controls. Regression analyses reveal that these effects are mainly driven by frequency rather than consistency in form-meaning mapping. Only when just friends share embeddings are the qualitative patterns of bilinguals reproduced. Overall, bilingual language models capture some cross-linguistic activation effects. However, their alignment with human processing seems to critically depend on how lexical overlap is encoded, possibly limiting their explanatory adequacy as models of bilingual reading.

</details>


### [83] [Multilingual Embedding Probes Fail to Generalize Across Learner Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07095)
*Laurits Lyngbaek,Ross Deans Kristensen-McLachlan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言嵌入模型是否编码了语言通用的熟练度表示？研究发现，虽然线性/非线性探针在分布内评估中能有效预测CEFR水平，但在跨语料库评估中性能崩溃，表明模型学习的是语料库特定的分布特性而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究当前多语言嵌入模型是否真正编码了语言通用的熟练度表示，这对于基于表示的熟练度自适应语言技术具有重要意义。研究者希望了解这些模型能否捕捉到跨语言、跨语料库的抽象熟练度维度。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）在Qwen3-Embedding（0.6B、4B、8B）的隐藏状态激活上训练线性和非线性探针；2）预测来自9个语料库、7种语言的二语学习者文本的CEFR熟练度水平；3）比较5种探针架构与基于表层文本特征的基线；4）进行分布内和跨语料库评估；5）通过残差分析探究探针学习到的映射特性。

Result: 研究结果显示：1）在分布内评估中，探针表现出色（QWK≈0.7），显著优于表层特征基线，且中间层预测效果最佳；2）但在跨语料库评估中，所有探针类型和模型大小的性能都崩溃；3）残差分析表明，分布外探针趋向于预测均匀分布的标签，说明学习到的映射捕捉的是语料库特定的分布特性（主题、语言、任务类型、评分方法），而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度。

Conclusion: 结论是当前多语言嵌入模型并未直接编码语言通用的熟练度表示。模型学习到的是语料库特定的分布特性，而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度。这一发现对基于表示的熟练度自适应语言技术方法具有重要启示，表明需要更谨慎地处理跨语料库的泛化问题。

Abstract: Do multilingual embedding models encode a language-general representation of proficiency? We investigate this by training linear and non-linear probes on hidden-state activations from Qwen3-Embedding (0.6B, 4B, 8B) to predict CEFR proficiency levels from learner texts across nine corpora and seven languages. We compare five probing architectures against a baseline trained on surface-level text features. Under in-distribution evaluation, probes achieve strong performance ($QWK\approx0.7$), substantially outperforming the surface baseline, with middle layers consistently yielding the best predictions. However, in cross-corpus evaluation performance collapses across all probe types and model sizes. Residual analysis reveals that out-of-distribution probes converge towards predicting uniformly distributed labels, indicating that the learned mappings capture corpus-specific distributional properties (topic, language, task type, rating methodology) rather than an abstract, transferable proficiency dimension. These results suggest that current multilingual embeddings do not straightforwardly encode language-general proficiency, with implications for representation-based approaches to proficiency-adaptive language technology.

</details>


### [84] [Evaluating In-Context Translation with Synchronous Context-Free Grammar Transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07320)
*Jackson Petty,Jaulie Goe,Tal Linzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在低资源语言翻译中面临数据依赖问题，研究通过形式语法任务评估LLMs基于上下文语法描述进行翻译的能力，发现语法规模、句子长度、形态和书写差异显著影响性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言翻译面临数据稀缺问题，LLMs依赖大量训练数据。研究探索LLMs能否利用上下文中的语言描述（如教科书、词典）进行翻译，这需要模型能够从语法描述中推断语言规则并应用于具体句子

Method: 构建同步上下文无关文法来定义形式语言对，模拟自然语言的语法、形态和书写特征。通过提供文法和源语言句子，测量LLMs将句子从一个形式语言翻译到另一个的能力。变量包括：文法规模、句子长度、语言间的句法和形态特性差异、书写系统差异

Result: 1. LLMs翻译准确率随文法规模和句子长度显著下降；2. 源语言和目标语言间的形态和书写差异会严重降低模型性能；3. 错误分析显示模型主要错误类型：错误回忆目标语言词汇、产生幻觉新词、未翻译源语言词汇

Conclusion: LLMs基于上下文语法描述进行翻译的能力有限，特别是在处理复杂文法、长句以及存在形态和书写差异的语言时。这表明当前LLMs在低资源语言翻译中利用语言描述的能力仍有局限，需要进一步改进

Abstract: Low-resource languages pose a challenge for machine translation with large language models (LLMs), which require large amounts of training data. One potential way to circumvent this data dependence is to rely on LLMs' ability to use in-context descriptions of languages, like textbooks and dictionaries. To do so, LLMs must be able to infer the link between the languages' grammatical descriptions and the sentences in question. Here we isolate this skill using a formal analogue of the task: string transduction based on a formal grammar provided in-context. We construct synchronous context-free grammars which define pairs of formal languages designed to model particular aspects of natural language grammar, morphology, and written representation. Using these grammars, we measure how well LLMs can translate sentences from one formal language into another when given both the grammar and the source-language sentence. We vary the size of the grammar, the lengths of the sentences, the syntactic and morphological properties of the languages, and their written script. We note three key findings. First, LLMs' translation accuracy decreases markedly as a function of grammar size and sentence length. Second, differences in morphology and written representation between the source and target languages can strongly diminish model performance. Third, we examine the types of errors committed by models and find they are most prone to recall the wrong words from the target language vocabulary, hallucinate new words, or leave source-language words untranslated.

</details>


### [85] [Yale-DM-Lab at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Deterministic Grounding and Multi-Pass Evidence Alignment for EHR Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07116)
*Elyas Irankhah,Samah Fodeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Yale-DM-Lab系统在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中采用多模型集成方法处理患者病历问答，包含四个子任务：问题重构、证据识别、答案生成和证据对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决患者对住院记录提出的问题，需要将患者语言转化为临床可解释的问题，并准确识别相关证据、生成答案、对齐证据与答案，以提高医疗问答系统的准确性和实用性。

Method: 采用分层多模型集成策略：ST1使用Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o双模型管道进行问题重构；ST2-ST4使用Azure托管的模型集成（o3、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.1、DeepSeek-R1），结合少样本提示和投票策略；在证据对齐任务中提供完整临床答案段落作为额外上下文。

Result: 实验显示：1）模型多样性和集成投票相比单模型基线持续提升性能；2）提供完整临床答案段落有助于证据对齐；3）对齐准确性主要受推理能力限制。开发集最佳得分：ST4微F1 88.81，ST2宏F1 65.72，ST3 34.01，ST1 33.05。

Conclusion: 多模型集成方法在医疗问答任务中有效，模型多样性是关键优势，但证据对齐任务仍受推理能力限制，未来需要改进推理机制以进一步提升对齐准确性。

Abstract: We describe the Yale-DM-Lab system for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task. The task studies patient-authored questions about hospitalization records and contains four subtasks (ST): clinician-interpreted question reformulation, evidence sentence identification, answer generation, and evidence-answer alignment. ST1 uses a dual-model pipeline with Claude Sonnet 4 and GPT-4o to reformulate patient questions into clinician-interpreted questions. ST2-ST4 rely on Azure-hosted model ensembles (o3, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.1, and DeepSeek-R1) combined with few-shot prompting and voting strategies. Our experiments show three main findings. First, model diversity and ensemble voting consistently improve performance compared to single-model baselines. Second, the full clinician answer paragraph is provided as additional prompt context for evidence alignment. Third, results on the development set show that alignment accuracy is mainly limited by reasoning. The best scores on the development set reach 88.81 micro F1 on ST4, 65.72 macro F1 on ST2, 34.01 on ST3, and 33.05 on ST1.

</details>


### [86] [Are Non-English Papers Reviewed Fairly? Language-of-Study Bias in NLP Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07119)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Abdulfattah Safa,Verena Blaschke,Erika Lombart,Marie-Catherine de Marneffe,Gözde Gül Şahin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统性地研究NLP同行评审中的语言研究偏见，区分负面和正面偏见形式，创建LOBSTER数据集，开发检测方法，分析15,645条评审，发现非英语论文面临更高偏见率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审在NLP发表过程中起核心作用，但容易受到各种偏见影响。本研究关注语言研究偏见：评审者基于论文研究的语言而非科学价值进行不同评价的倾向。尽管评审指南明确禁止此类偏见，但对其理解仍很有限

Method: 1. 首次系统性地定义语言研究偏见，区分负面和正面形式；2. 创建人工标注数据集LOBSTER；3. 开发检测方法，达到87.37的宏观F1分数；4. 分析15,645条评审数据；5. 识别负面偏见的四个子类别

Result: 1. 非英语论文面临的语言研究偏见率显著高于仅研究英语的论文；2. 负面偏见始终超过正面偏见；3. 识别出四种负面偏见子类别，其中要求不合理的跨语言泛化是最主要形式；4. 检测方法达到87.37宏观F1分数

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统性地表征了语言研究偏见，提供了数据集和检测方法，揭示了非英语论文面临的不公平评审现象，为NLP及其他领域建立更公平的评审实践提供了支持资源

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in the NLP publication process, but is susceptible to various biases. Here, we study language-of-study (LoS) bias: the tendency for reviewers to evaluate a paper differently based on the language(s) it studies, rather than its scientific merit. Despite being explicitly flagged in reviewing guidelines, such biases are poorly understood. Prior work treats such comments as part of broader categories of weak or unconstructive reviews without defining them as a distinct form of bias. We present the first systematic characterization of LoS bias, distinguishing negative and positive forms, and introduce the human-annotated dataset LOBSTER (Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review) and a method achieving 87.37 macro F1 for detection. We analyze 15,645 reviews to estimate how negative and positive biases differ with respect to the LoS, and find that non-English papers face substantially higher bias rates than English-only ones, with negative bias consistently outweighing positive bias. Finally, we identify four subcategories of negative bias, and find that demanding unjustified cross-lingual generalization is the most dominant form. We publicly release all resources to support work on fairer reviewing practices in NLP and beyond.

</details>


### [87] [Language Bias under Conflicting Information in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07123)
*Robert Östling,Murathan Kurfalı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在处理多语言冲突信息时存在语言偏好偏见，倾向于忽略冲突并自信地选择特定语言的答案，普遍对俄语有偏见，在长上下文场景下偏好中文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究LLMs在整合冲突信息时是否不仅存在内容偏见，还存在语言偏见。具体来说，当不同语言提供相互矛盾的信息时，LLMs是否会偏向特定语言而忽略冲突。

Method: 将"干草堆中的冲突针"范式扩展到多语言设置，使用五种不同语言的新闻领域自然数据，对多种不同规模的多语言LLMs进行全面评估，包括GPT-5.2等模型。

Result: 所有测试的LLMs在大多数情况下都忽略冲突并自信地断言仅一种可能答案。存在一致的语言偏好偏见：普遍对俄语有偏见，在最长上下文长度场景下偏好中文。这些模式在中国境内和境外训练的模型中均存在，但在前者中更为明显。

Conclusion: LLMs在处理多语言冲突信息时确实存在语言偏见，模型倾向于忽略信息冲突而选择特定语言的答案，这种偏见在不同训练来源的模型中普遍存在，需要进一步研究和缓解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to contain biases in the process of integrating conflicting information when answering questions. Here we ask whether such biases also exist with respect to which language is used for each conflicting piece of information. To answer this question, we extend the conflicting needles in a haystack paradigm to a multilingual setting and perform a comprehensive set of evaluations with naturalistic news domain data in five different languages, for a range of multilingual LLMs of different sizes. We find that all LLMs tested, including GPT-5.2, ignore the conflict and confidently assert only one of the possible answers in the large majority of cases. Furthermore, there is a consistent bias across models in which languages are preferred, with a general bias against Russian and, for the longest context lengths, in favor of Chinese. Both of these patterns are consistent between models trained inside and outside of mainland China, though somewhat stronger in the former category.

</details>


### [88] [LaScA: Language-Conditioned Scalable Modelling of Affective Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07193)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种使用语言模型作为语义上下文调节器的新型情感预测框架，将手工特征转换为符号化自然语言描述，在保持可解释性的同时提升预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度神经嵌入的情感预测方法在无约束环境中缺乏可解释性，限制了专家驱动的优化。需要一种既能保持特征透明度又能利用语言模型上下文抽象能力的方法

Method: 从结构化领域知识中提取可解释的面部几何和声学特征，将其转换为编码情感含义的符号化自然语言描述，使用预训练语言模型处理这些描述生成语义上下文嵌入，作为情感动态的高层先验

Result: 在Aff-Wild2和SEWA数据集上的实验表明，该方法在效价和唤醒度的预测准确性上均优于纯手工特征和深度嵌入基线，实现了可解释性与预测性能的平衡

Conclusion: 语义上下文调节能够在保持特征透明度的同时提升情感建模性能，为完全端到端的黑盒架构提供了透明且计算高效的替代方案

Abstract: Predicting affect in unconstrained environments remains a fundamental challenge in human-centered AI. While deep neural embeddings dominate contemporary approaches, they often lack interpretability and limit expert-driven refinement. We propose a novel framework that uses Language Models (LMs) as semantic context conditioners over handcrafted affect descriptors to model changes in Valence and Arousal. Our approach begins with interpretable facial geometry and acoustic features derived from structured domain knowledge. These features are transformed into symbolic natural-language descriptions encoding their affective implications. A pretrained LM processes these descriptions to generate semantic context embeddings that act as high-level priors over affective dynamics. Unlike end-to-end black-box pipelines, our framework preserves feature transparency while leveraging the contextual abstraction capabilities of LMs. We evaluate the proposed method on the Aff-Wild2 and SEWA datasets for affect change prediction. Experimental results show consistent improvements in accuracy for both Valence and Arousal compared to handcrafted-only and deep-embedding baselines. Our findings demonstrate that semantic conditioning enables interpretable affect modelling without sacrificing predictive performance, offering a transparent and computationally efficient alternative to fully end-to-end architectures

</details>


### [89] [Efficient Learned Data Compression via Dual-Stream Feature Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07239)
*Huidong Ma,Xinyan Shi,Hui Sun,Xiaofei Yue,Xiaoguang Liu,Gang Wang,Wentong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双流多尺度解耦器，通过分离局部和全局上下文，用浅层并行流替代深度串行处理，并结合分层门控细化器实现自适应特征优化，同时设计并发流并行流水线克服系统瓶颈，在压缩比和吞吐量上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型数据压缩（LDC）虽然取得了优越的压缩比，但平衡精确概率建模与系统效率仍然具有挑战性。统一的单流架构难以同时捕获微观句法和宏观语义特征，需要深度串行堆叠，这会加剧延迟。此外，异构系统受限于设备速度不匹配，串行处理导致吞吐量受阿姆达尔定律限制。

Method: 1. 双流多尺度解耦器：解耦局部和全局上下文，用浅层并行流替代深度串行处理；2. 分层门控细化器：实现自适应特征细化和精确概率建模；3. 并发流并行流水线：克服系统瓶颈，实现全流水线并行。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在压缩比和吞吐量方面均达到最先进性能，同时保持最低延迟和内存使用。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 通过解耦局部和全局上下文处理，采用并行架构替代传统串行堆叠，成功解决了学习型数据压缩中平衡压缩效率与系统性能的挑战，实现了压缩比、吞吐量和延迟的全面优化。

Abstract: While Learned Data Compression (LDC) has achieved superior compression ratios, balancing precise probability modeling with system efficiency remains challenging. Crucially, uniform single-stream architectures struggle to simultaneously capture micro-syntactic and macro-semantic features, necessitating deep serial stacking that exacerbates latency. Compounding this, heterogeneous systems are constrained by device speed mismatches, where throughput is capped by Amdahl's Law due to serial processing. To this end, we propose a Dual-Stream Multi-Scale Decoupler that disentangles local and global contexts to replace deep serial processing with shallow parallel streams, and incorporate a Hierarchical Gated Refiner for adaptive feature refinement and precise probability modeling. Furthermore, we design a Concurrent Stream-Parallel Pipeline, which overcomes systemic bottlenecks to achieve full-pipeline parallelism. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both compression ratio and throughput, while maintaining the lowest latency and memory usage. The code is available at https://github.com/huidong-ma/FADE.

</details>


### [90] [ClickGuard: A Trustworthy Adaptive Fusion Framework for Clickbait Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07272)
*Chhavi Dhiman,Naman Chawla,Riya Dhami,Gaurav Kumar,Ganesh Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClickGuard是一个用于点击诱饵检测的可信自适应融合框架，结合BERT嵌入和结构特征，通过句法-语义自适应融合块实现动态集成，在测试中达到96.93%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点击诱饵标题的广泛使用对在线可信度构成重大挑战，这些标题采用煽情、误导性声明和模糊语言，需要有效的检测方法来确保可信的数字内容。

Method: 提出ClickGuard框架，结合BERT嵌入和结构特征，使用句法-语义自适应融合块(SSAFB)进行动态集成，采用混合CNN-BiLSTM架构捕捉模式和依赖关系。

Result: 模型达到96.93%的测试准确率，优于现有最先进方法。通过LIME和置换特征重要性(PFI)评估可解释性和鲁棒性，消融研究验证了SSAFB的有效性。

Conclusion: ClickGuard为增强在线内容可信度提供了可扩展、可靠的解决方案，通过解决句法-语义建模挑战，在不同数据集上表现出稳健性能。

Abstract: The widespread use of clickbait headlines, crafted to mislead and maximize engagement, poses a significant challenge to online credibility. These headlines employ sensationalism, misleading claims, and vague language, underscoring the need for effective detection to ensure trustworthy digital content. The paper introduces, ClickGuard: a trustworthy adaptive fusion framework for clickbait detection. It combines BERT embeddings and structural features using a Syntactic-Semantic Adaptive Fusion Block (SSAFB) for dynamic integration. The framework incorporates a hybrid CNN-BiLSTM to capture patterns and dependencies. The model achieved 96.93% testing accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. The model's trustworthiness is evaluated using LIME and Permutation Feature Importance (PFI) for interpretability and perturbation analysis. These methods assess the model's robustness and sensitivity to feature changes by measuring the average prediction variation. Ablation studies validated the SSAFB's effectiveness in optimizing feature fusion. The model demonstrated robust performance across diverse datasets, providing a scalable, reliable solution for enhancing online content credibility by addressing syntactic-semantic modelling challenges. Code of the work is available at: https://github.com/palindromeRice/ClickBait_Detection_Architecture

</details>


### [91] [Why teaching resists automation in an AI-inundated era: Human judgment, non-modular work, and the limits of delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07285)
*Songhee Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文认为教学工作的本质特性使其难以被AI完全自动化，因为教学是解释性、关系性和基于专业判断的活动，依赖于对人类认知、行为、动机和社会互动的持续理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前关于AI在教育中应用的讨论往往将教学描绘为模块化和程序化工作、认为可以自动化或委托给技术的观点，作者提出反驳，认为这种观点错误地将教学视为可分离的活动。

Method: 基于近期文献和对大型语言模型、检索增强生成系统的实证研究，分析教学工作的本质特性及其与AI自动化能力的关系。

Result: AI虽然可以支持某些限定功能，但教学工作因其固有的解释性、关系性和专业判断特性而难以有意义地自动化；教学和学习受到人类认知、行为、动机和社会互动的影响，这些因素无法完全规定、预测或详尽建模。

Conclusion: 只要教育实践依赖于对人类认知和学习的涌现性理解，教学就仍然是一种抵抗自动化的专业工作；AI可以改善信息获取并支持特定教学活动，但不能取代有效教学所需的人类判断和关系责任。

Abstract: Debates about artificial intelligence (AI) in education often portray teaching as a modular and procedural job that can increasingly be automated or delegated to technology. This brief communication paper argues that such claims depend on treating teaching as more separable than it is in practice. Drawing on recent literature and empirical studies of large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems, I argue that although AI can support some bounded functions, instructional work remains difficult to automate in meaningful ways because it is inherently interpretive, relational, and grounded in professional judgment. More fundamentally, teaching and learning are shaped by human cognition, behavior, motivation, and social interaction in ways that cannot be fully specified, predicted, or exhaustively modeled. Tasks that may appear separable in principle derive their instructional value in practice from ongoing contextual interpretation across learners, situations, and relationships. As long as educational practice relies on emergent understanding of human cognition and learning, teaching remains a form of professional work that resists automation. AI may improve access to information and support selected instructional activities, but it does not remove the need for human judgment and relational accountability that effective teaching requires.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [SALLIE: Safeguarding Against Latent Language & Image Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06247)
*Guy Azov,Ofer Rivlin,Guy Shtar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SALLIE是一个轻量级运行时检测框架，通过分析模型内部激活来统一防御文本和视觉越狱攻击，无需架构修改且不降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法存在两个主要问题：1）复杂的输入转换会降低模型性能；2）将多模态威胁视为孤立问题。需要一种统一的、模态无关的防御方案，能够同时缓解文本和视觉威胁，且不降低性能或需要架构修改。

Method: SALLIE基于机制可解释性，集成到标准token级融合管道中。采用三阶段架构：1）提取内部残差流激活；2）使用K近邻分类器计算层级恶意分数；3）通过层集成模块聚合预测结果。

Result: 在Phi-3.5-vision-instruct、SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct和gemma-3-4b-it等开源架构上评估，涵盖10多个数据集和5个强基线方法。SALLIE在各种实验设置中始终优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SALLIE提供了一个统一、模态无关的防御框架，能够有效检测和缓解文本与视觉越狱攻击，同时保持模型性能，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain highly vulnerable to textual and visual jailbreaks, as well as prompt injections (arXiv:2307.15043, Greshake et al., 2023, arXiv:2306.13213). Existing defenses often degrade performance through complex input transformations or treat multimodal threats as isolated problems (arXiv:2309.00614, arXiv:2310.03684, Zhang et al., 2025). To address the critical gap for a unified, modal-agnostic defense that mitigates both textual and visual threats simultaneously without degrading performance or requiring architectural modifications, we introduce SALLIE (Safeguarding Against Latent Language & Image Exploits), a lightweight runtime detection framework rooted in mechanistic interpretability (Lindsey et al., 2025, Ameisen et al., 2025). By integrating seamlessly into standard token-level fusion pipelines (arXiv:2306.13549), SALLIE extracts robust signals directly from the model's internal activations. At inference, SALLIE defends via a three-stage architecture: (1) extracting internal residual stream activations, (2) calculating layer-wise maliciousness scores using a K-Nearest Neighbors (k-NN) classifier, and (3) aggregating these predictions via a layer ensemble module. We evaluate SALLIE on compact, open-source architectures - Phi-3.5-vision-instruct (arXiv:2404.14219), SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct (arXiv:2504.05299), and gemma-3-4b-it (arXiv:2503.19786) - prioritized for practical inference times and real-world deployment costs. Our comprehensive evaluation pipeline spans over ten datasets and more than five strong baseline methods from the literature, and SALLIE consistently outperforms these baselines across a wide range of experimental settings.

</details>


### [93] [Zero Trust in the Context of IoT: Industrial Literature Review, Trends, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06272)
*Laurent Bobelin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对零信任模型与物联网设备集成的研究现状进行文献综述，分析学术研究、行业实践与标准之间的差距，识别当前知识、趋势和未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任模型日益流行，但物联网/智能外围设备的集成仍存在研究空白。NIST标准未明确具体实施细节，且行业中存在大量标榜"零信任物联网集成"但实际不符合标准的情况。需要整合学术研究、行业实践和标准，以明确当前知识状态和解决方案。

Method: 通过对非学术出版物进行文献综述，比较行业实践导向文献与学术工作和标准，整合当前知识、趋势和解决方案。

Result: 识别了零信任与物联网集成的当前知识状态、行业趋势和未来挑战，揭示了学术研究、行业实践与标准之间的差距，特别是物联网设备特殊性（低能耗/计算能力、生命周期、使用方式）带来的集成困难。

Conclusion: 零信任模型与物联网设备集成仍面临诸多未解决的研究挑战，需要进一步整合学术研究、行业实践和标准，以实现有效的工业集成。本文为理解当前状态和未来研究方向提供了基础。

Abstract: The Zero-trust (ZT) model is an increasingly popular model that relies on the idea that no trust should be granted to any entity (network, persons, devices) by default. ZT model is gaining attention from both research and practice, with various levels of adequation between research developed and real-life applications. NIST provided a standard to fulfill requirements of ZT architecture of network core but many practical aspects remain unspecified, some of them requiring solving first research challenges in order to be implemented efficiently. An example of such an unspecified field is the integration of IoT/Smart Peripheral Devices (SPD). Various reasons explain this gap: specificities of such resources (possibly lower energy/computation power), their lifecycle, and their use, strongly depending on the use of the whole platform IoT devices are part of. Moreover, additional difficulty to have a good understanding is induced by the fact that both Zero Trust and IoT are identified as promising trends in cybersecurity: many vendors/researchers tag their solutions as IoT integration into the ZT model, with little to no effective compliance to ZT model or standard. Industry is providing many practice-oriented literature, that has to be compared to academic work and standards, in order to consolidate the current state of knowledge and solutions offered to realize this integration. In this paper, we conduct a literature review of non-academic publications, in order to consolidate current knowledge, trends, and future challenges for the industrial integration of IoT devices in ZT architecture.

</details>


### [94] [Harnessing Hyperbolic Geometry for Harmful Prompt Detection and Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06285)
*Igor Maljkovic,Maria Rosaria Briglia,Iacopo Masi,Antonio Emanuele Cinà,Fabio Roli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HyPE和HyPS框架，利用双曲空间几何特性检测和净化恶意提示，保护视觉语言模型安全


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽然功能强大，但其灵活性使其容易受到恶意提示攻击，产生不安全内容。现有防御方法（黑名单过滤或重型分类器）存在易规避、成本高、脆弱等问题，特别是在嵌入层攻击下表现不佳。

Method: 提出两个互补组件：1）Hyperbolic Prompt Espial (HyPE)：轻量级异常检测器，利用双曲空间的结构化几何特性建模良性提示，将有害提示检测为异常值；2）Hyperbolic Prompt Sanitization (HyPS)：基于检测结果，应用可解释归因方法识别并选择性修改有害词汇，在保留用户提示原始语义的同时消除不安全意图。

Result: 在多个数据集和对抗场景下的广泛实验表明，该框架在检测准确性和鲁棒性方面始终优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: HyPE和HyPS共同提供了一个高效、可解释且具有弹性的方法，能够有效保护视觉语言模型免受恶意提示滥用。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have become essential for tasks such as image synthesis, captioning, and retrieval by aligning textual and visual information in a shared embedding space. Yet, this flexibility also makes them vulnerable to malicious prompts designed to produce unsafe content, raising critical safety concerns. Existing defenses either rely on blacklist filters, which are easily circumvented, or on heavy classifier-based systems, both of which are costly and fragile under embedding-level attacks. We address these challenges with two complementary components: Hyperbolic Prompt Espial (HyPE) and Hyperbolic Prompt Sanitization (HyPS). HyPE is a lightweight anomaly detector that leverages the structured geometry of hyperbolic space to model benign prompts and detect harmful ones as outliers. HyPS builds on this detection by applying explainable attribution methods to identify and selectively modify harmful words, neutralizing unsafe intent while preserving the original semantics of user prompts. Through extensive experiments across multiple datasets and adversarial scenarios, we prove that our framework consistently outperforms prior defenses in both detection accuracy and robustness. Together, HyPE and HyPS offer an efficient, interpretable, and resilient approach to safeguarding VLMs against malicious prompt misuse.

</details>


### [95] [Adversarial Robustness of Time-Series Classification for Crystal Collimator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06289)
*Xaver Fink,Borja Fernandez Adiego,Daniele Mirarchi,Eloise Matheson,Alvaro Garcia Gonzales,Gianmarco Ricci,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析和改进了用于CERN大型强子对撞机晶体准直器对准的CNN分类器的对抗鲁棒性，通过构建预处理感知包装器并利用对抗微调，将鲁棒准确率提升了18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是提高用于CERN大型强子对撞机晶体准直器对准的CNN时间序列分类器的对抗鲁棒性。该分类器通过分析光束损失监测器时间序列来辅助晶体旋转对准，需要在实际部署环境中抵御基于真实世界可行性的对抗攻击。

Method: 方法包括：1) 形式化基于真实世界可行性的对抗威胁模型下的局部鲁棒性属性；2) 构建预处理感知包装器，将时间序列归一化、填充约束和结构化扰动编码为CNN前的轻量级可微分包装器；3) 使用Foolbox和ART框架进行攻击基准测试；4) 对CNN进行对抗微调；5) 将鲁棒性扩展到滑动窗口分类的序列级鲁棒性。

Result: 对抗微调使CNN的鲁棒准确率提升了高达18.6%，同时不降低干净准确率。研究还发现了攻击诱导的误分类在相邻窗口间持续存在的现象，并生成了对抗序列作为全扫描时间鲁棒性要求的反例。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提高了CERN大型强子对撞机晶体准直器对准CNN分类器的对抗鲁棒性，通过预处理感知包装器和对抗微调实现了显著的鲁棒性改进。研究还扩展了时间序列数据的鲁棒性概念，从单窗口扩展到序列级鲁棒性，为实际部署的机器学习系统提供了重要的安全增强方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze and improve the adversarial robustness of a convolutional neural network (CNN) that assists crystal-collimator alignment at CERN's Large Hadron Collider (LHC) by classifying a beam-loss monitor (BLM) time series during crystal rotation. We formalize a local robustness property for this classifier under an adversarial threat model based on real-world plausibility. Building on established parameterized input-transformation patterns used for transformation- and semantic-perturbation robustness, we instantiate a preprocessing-aware wrapper for our deployed time-series pipeline: we encode time-series normalization, padding constraints, and structured perturbations as a lightweight differentiable wrapper in front of the CNN, so that existing gradient-based robustness frameworks can operate on the deployed pipeline. For formal verification, data-dependent preprocessing such as per-window z-normalization introduces nonlinear operators that require verifier-specific abstractions. We therefore focus on attack-based robustness estimates and pipeline-checked validity by benchmarking robustness with the frameworks Foolbox and ART. Adversarial fine-tuning of the resulting CNN improves robust accuracy by up to 18.6 % without degrading clean accuracy. Finally, we extend robustness on time-series data beyond single windows to sequence-level robustness for sliding-window classification, introduce adversarial sequences as counterexamples to a temporal robustness requirement over full scans, and observe attack-induced misclassifications that persist across adjacent windows.

</details>


### [96] [FedSpy-LLM: Towards Scalable and Generalizable Data Reconstruction Attacks from Gradients on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06297)
*Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Feiyi Wang,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedSpy-LLM：一种针对联邦学习大语言模型的可扩展通用数据重建攻击方法，能够处理更大批次和更长序列，并适用于多种模型架构和参数高效微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管联邦学习结合参数高效微调在保护隐私方面具有优势，但先前的研究表明私有数据仍可能从共享梯度中被提取。现有攻击方法主要针对全参数模型训练，且仅限于小批次、短输入序列和特定模型架构，在PEFT方法下的重建质量更差。需要全面理解联邦LLMs的实际攻击面。

Method: 提出FedSpy-LLM攻击框架，核心是新颖的梯度分解策略，利用梯度的秩不足和子空间结构，实现高效令牌提取并保留关键信号成分。通过迭代对齐每个令牌的部分序列梯度与完整序列梯度，确保重建序列中令牌顺序的准确性。

Result: 该方法能够重建更大批次和更长序列的训练数据，并泛化到多种模型架构（编码器、解码器、编码器-解码器），即使在部署PEFT方法进行训练时也能保持鲁棒性，克服了PEFT引入的大量零空间带来的重建挑战。

Conclusion: FedSpy-LLM揭示了联邦LLMs的实际安全风险，表明即使采用PEFT方法，数据重建攻击仍然可行且有效，为联邦学习系统的安全设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Given the growing reliance on private data in training Large Language Models (LLMs), Federated Learning (FL) combined with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has garnered significant attention for enhancing privacy and efficiency. Despite FL's privacy benefits, prior studies have shown that private data can still be extracted from shared gradients. However, these studies, mainly on full-parameter model training, are limited to reconstructing small batches, short input sequences, and specific model architectures, such as encoder-based or decoder-based models. The reconstruction quality becomes even worse when dealing with gradients from PEFT methods. To fully understand the practical attack surface of federated LLMs, this paper proposes FedSpy-LLM, a scalable and generalizable data reconstruction attack designed to reconstruct training data with larger batch sizes and longer sequences while generalizing across diverse model architectures, even when PEFT methods are deployed for training. At the core of FedSpy-LLM is a novel gradient decomposition strategy that exploits the rank deficiency and subspace structure of gradients, enabling efficient token extraction while preserving key signal components at scale. This approach further mitigates the reconstruction challenges introduced by PEFT's substantial null space, ensuring robustness across encoder-based, decoder-based, and encoder-decoder model architectures. Additionally, by iteratively aligning each token's partial-sequence gradient with the full-sequence gradient, FedSpy-LLM ensures accurate token ordering in reconstructed sequences.

</details>


### [97] [The Defense Trilemma: Why Prompt Injection Defense Wrappers Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06436)
*Manish Bhatt,Sarthak Munshi,Vineeth Sai Narajala,Idan Habler,Ammar Al-Kahfah,Ken Huang,Blake Gatto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文证明：对于具有连通提示空间的LLM，不存在能同时满足连续性、效用保持和完全安全性的包装防御方法，形成了防御三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是分析包装防御（在模型处理前预处理输入的函数）的理论局限性，探讨是否可能设计出既能保持效用又能确保所有输出完全安全的防御机制。

Method: 采用形式化数学证明方法，在逐步增强的假设下建立三个主要结果：边界固定定理、ε-鲁棒约束定理和持久不安全区域定理。同时扩展到离散情况、多轮交互、随机防御和容量对等设置，并在Lean 4中进行机械验证。

Result: 证明了防御三难困境：连续性、效用保持和完全安全性无法同时实现。具体表现为：1）防御必须保留某些阈值级输入不变；2）在Lipschitz正则性下，固定边界点周围的带区域保持接近阈值；3）在横截性条件下，存在正测度子集保持严格不安全。

Conclusion: 包装防御存在根本性理论限制，无法同时实现连续性、效用保持和完全安全性。这并不排除训练时对齐、架构修改或牺牲效用的防御方法。研究结果为LLM安全防御设计提供了重要的理论边界。

Abstract: We prove that no continuous, utility-preserving wrapper defense-a function $D: X\to X$ that preprocesses inputs before the model sees them-can make all outputs strictly safe for a language model with connected prompt space, and we characterize exactly where every such defense must fail. We establish three results under successively stronger hypotheses: boundary fixation-the defense must leave some threshold-level inputs unchanged; an $ε$-robust constraint-under Lipschitz regularity, a positive-measure band around fixed boundary points remains near-threshold; and a persistent unsafe region under a transversality condition, a positive-measure subset of inputs remains strictly unsafe. These constitute a defense trilemma: continuity, utility preservation, and completeness cannot coexist. We prove parallel discrete results requiring no topology, and extend to multi-turn interactions, stochastic defenses, and capacity-parity settings. The results do not preclude training-time alignment, architectural changes, or defenses that sacrifice utility. The full theory is mechanically verified in Lean 4 and validated empirically on three LLMs.

</details>


### [98] [Guiding Symbolic Execution with Static Analysis and LLMs for Vulnerability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06506)
*Md Shafiuzzaman,Achintya Desai,Wenbo Guo,Tevfik Bultan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAILOR自动化符号执行harness构建，通过结合静态分析和LLM合成，在6.8M行C/C++代码中发现379个未知内存安全漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号执行需要专家手动编写harness来设置符号状态、建模依赖和指定断言，这限制了该技术在大规模代码库中的应用可扩展性

Method: 三阶段方法：1) 静态分析识别候选漏洞位置并生成漏洞规范；2) LLM使用漏洞规范并通过迭代精炼驱动程序、存根和断言来编排harness合成；3) 具体执行验证符号执行结果

Result: 在10个开源C/C++项目(6.8M行代码)中发现379个不同的先前未知内存安全漏洞(421个确认崩溃)。最强基线(Claude Code)仅发现12个漏洞

Conclusion: SAILOR结合了静态分析的可扩展性、LLM的代码推理能力、符号执行的路径精度和具体执行的地面真值，显著提高了漏洞检测的自动化程度和效果

Abstract: Symbolic execution detects vulnerabilities with precision, but applying it to large codebases requires harnesses that set up symbolic state, model dependencies, and specify assertions. Writing these harnesses has traditionally been a manual process requiring expert knowledge, which significantly limits the scalability of the technique. We present Static Analysis Informed and LLM-Orchestrated Symbolic Execution (SAILOR), which automates symbolic execution harness construction by combining static analysis with LLM-based synthesis. SAILOR operates in three phases: (1) static analysis identifies candidate vulnerable locations and generates vulnerability specifications; (2) an LLM uses vulnerability specifications and orchestrates harness synthesis by iteratively refining drivers, stubs, and assertions against compiler and symbolic execution feedback; symbolic execution then detects vulnerabilities using the generated harness, and (3) concrete replay validates the symbolic execution results against the unmodified project source. This design combines the scalability of static analysis, the code reasoning of LLMs, the path precision of symbolic execution, and the ground truth produced by concrete execution. We evaluate SAILOR on 10 open-source C/C++ projects totaling 6.8 M lines of code. SAILOR discovers 379 distinct, previously unknown memory-safety vulnerabilities (421 confirmed crashes). The strongest of five baselines we compare SAILOR to (agentic vulnerability detection using Claude Code with full codebase access and unlimited interaction), finds only 12 vulnerabilities. Each phase of SAILOR is critical: Without static analysis targeting confirmed vulnerabilities drop 12.2X; without iterative LLM synthesis zero vulnerabilities are confirmed; and without symbolic execution no approach can detect more than 12 vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [99] [SkillSieve: A Hierarchical Triage Framework for Detecting Malicious AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06550)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillSieve是一个三层检测框架，用于识别OpenClaw ClawHub市场中社区贡献的agent技能中的安全漏洞，包括提示注入和社会工程攻击，通过渐进式分析在保持低成本的同时实现高检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw的ClawHub市场托管了超过13,000个社区贡献的agent技能，其中13%-26%存在安全漏洞。传统的正则表达式扫描器无法检测混淆的有效载荷，而形式化静态分析器无法读取SKILL.md文件中的自然语言指令，这些指令中隐藏着提示注入和社会工程攻击。现有方法都无法同时处理这两种模态。

Method: SkillSieve采用三层渐进式检测框架：第一层使用基于XGBoost的特征评分器运行正则表达式、AST和元数据检查，以零API成本在40ms内过滤约86%的良性技能；第二层将可疑技能发送给LLM，将分析拆分为四个并行子任务（意图对齐、权限合理性、隐蔽行为检测、跨文件一致性），每个任务有自己的提示和结构化输出；第三层将高风险技能提交给三个不同LLM组成的陪审团，独立投票并在意见分歧时进行辩论后达成裁决。

Result: 在49,592个真实ClawHub技能和对抗样本上评估，使用五种规避技术，在440 ARM单板计算机上运行完整流水线。在400个技能标记基准测试中，SkillSieve达到0.800 F1分数，优于ClawVet的0.421，平均每个技能成本为0.006美元。

Conclusion: SkillSieve通过渐进式分析框架有效解决了agent技能安全检测中多模态挑战，在保持低成本的同时显著提高了检测性能，代码、数据和基准测试已开源。

Abstract: OpenClaw's ClawHub marketplace hosts over 13,000 community-contributed agent skills, and between 13% and 26% of them contain security vulnerabilities according to recent audits. Regex scanners miss obfuscated payloads; formal static analyzers cannot read the natural language instructions in SKILL.md files where prompt injection and social engineering attacks hide. Neither approach handles both modalities. SkillSieve is a three-layer detection framework that applies progressively deeper analysis only where needed. Layer 1 runs regex, AST, and metadata checks through an XGBoost-based feature scorer, filtering roughly 86% of benign skills in under 40ms on average at zero API cost. Layer 2 sends suspicious skills to an LLM, but instead of asking one broad question, it splits the analysis into four parallel sub-tasks (intent alignment, permission justification, covert behavior detection, cross-file consistency), each with its own prompt and structured output. Layer 3 puts high-risk skills before a jury of three different LLMs that vote independently and, if they disagree, debate before reaching a verdict. We evaluate on 49,592 real ClawHub skills and adversarial samples across five evasion techniques, running the full pipeline on a 440 ARM single-board computer. On a 400-skill labeled benchmark, SkillSieve achieves 0.800 F1, outperforming ClawVet's 0.421, at an average cost of 0.006 per skill. Code, data, and benchmark are open-sourced.

</details>


### [100] [Can Drift-Adaptive Malware Detectors Be Made Robust? Attacks and Defenses Under White-Box and Black-Box Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06599)
*Adrian Shuai Li,Md Ajwad Akil,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AdvDA框架通过对抗性领域适应解决概念漂移和对抗性规避的组合挑战，提出通用鲁棒化框架提升恶意软件检测器在分布变化下的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念漂移和对抗性规避是恶意软件检测器部署中的两大挑战，但现有研究通常单独处理这两个问题。两者的组合——漂移自适应检测器的对抗鲁棒性——尚未被探索。AdvDA恶意软件检测器使用对抗性领域适应来对齐带标签的源域和标签有限的目标域，但分布偏移带来了独特挑战：在源域学习的鲁棒性可能无法迁移到目标域，而现有防御方法假设固定分布。

Method: 提出通用鲁棒化框架，在对抗性变换的输入上微调预训练的AdvDA模型，该框架与攻击类型和变换选择无关。实例化了五种防御变体，涵盖两种威胁模型：特征空间中的白盒PGD攻击，以及通过功能保持的控制流突变修改恶意软件二进制文件的黑盒MalGuise攻击。在九种防御配置、五个月度适应窗口和三个误报率操作点上进行评估。

Result: 未防御的AdvDA对PGD攻击完全脆弱（100%攻击成功率），对MalGuise攻击中等脆弱（13%）。提出的框架将这些比率分别降低至最低3.2%和5.1%。但最优策略不同：源对抗训练对PGD防御至关重要，但对MalGuise防御适得其反，后者仅需目标训练即可。此外，鲁棒性在这两种威胁模型之间无法迁移。

Conclusion: 概念漂移和对抗性规避的组合挑战需要专门解决方案。提出的通用鲁棒化框架能有效提升漂移自适应检测器的对抗鲁棒性，但不同威胁模型需要不同的防御策略。研究提供了平衡鲁棒性、检测准确性和计算成本的部署建议。

Abstract: Concept drift and adversarial evasion are two major challenges for deploying machine learning-based malware detectors. While both have been studied separately, their combination, the adversarial robustness of drift-adaptive detectors, remains unexplored. We address this problem with AdvDA, a recent malware detector that uses adversarial domain adaptation to align a labeled source domain with a target domain with limited labels. The distribution shift between domains poses a unique challenge: robustness learned on the source may not transfer to the target, and existing defenses assume a fixed distribution. To address this, we propose a universal robustification framework that fine-tunes a pretrained AdvDA model on adversarially transformed inputs, agnostic to the attack type and choice of transformations. We instantiate it with five defense variants spanning two threat models: white-box PGD attacks in the feature space and black-box MalGuise attacks that modify malware binaries via functionality-preserving control-flow mutations. Across nine defense configurations, five monthly adaptation windows on Windows malware, and three false-positive-rate operating points, we find the undefended AdvDA completely vulnerable to PGD (100% attack success) and moderately to MalGuise (13%). Our framework reduces these rates to as low as 3.2% and 5.1%, respectively, but the optimal strategy differs: source adversarial training is essential for PGD defenses yet counterproductive for MalGuise defenses, where target-only training suffices. Furthermore, robustness does not transfer across these two threat models. We provide deployment recommendations that balance robustness, detection accuracy, and computational cost.

</details>


### [101] [Argus: Reorchestrating Static Analysis via a Multi-Agent Ensemble for Full-Chain Security Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06633)
*Zi Liang,Qipeng Xie,Jun He,Bohuan Xue,Weizheng Wang,Yuandao Cai,Fei Luo,Boxian Zhang,Haibo Hu,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Argus是一个专为漏洞检测设计的首个多智能体框架，通过重新编排SAST工作流程，采用检索增强生成和多智能体协作，显著提升了漏洞检测效果并降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的静态应用安全测试方法试图直接替代人类专家，但缺乏与现有SAST工具的有效集成，导致误报率高、幻觉问题、推理深度有限和令牌使用过多等问题，难以在工业部署中实用。

Method: 提出了从LLM辅助结构到LLM中心化工作流程的范式转变，设计了Argus多智能体框架，包含三个关键创新：全面的供应链分析、协作的多智能体工作流程，以及集成检索增强生成和ReAct等最先进技术来最小化幻觉并增强推理能力。

Result: 广泛的实证评估表明，Argus显著优于现有方法，能够检测出更多真实漏洞，同时减少误报和运营成本。值得注意的是，Argus已经识别出多个获得CVE分配的关键零日漏洞。

Conclusion: Argus通过重新编排SAST工作流程并采用多智能体框架，成功解决了现有LLM-based SAST方法的局限性，为工业级漏洞检测提供了实用有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have sparked interest in their application to Static Application Security Testing (SAST), primarily due to their superior contextual reasoning capabilities compared to traditional symbolic or rule-based methods. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically attempt to replace human experts directly without integrating effectively with existing SAST tools. This lack of integration results in ineffectiveness, including high rates of false positives, hallucinations, limited reasoning depth, and excessive token usage, making them impractical for industrial deployment. To overcome these limitations, we present a paradigm shift that reorchestrates the SAST workflow from current LLM-assisted structure to a new LLM-centered workflow. We introduce Argus (Agentic and Retrieval-Augmented Guarding System), the first multi-agent framework designed specifically for vulnerability detection. Argus incorporates three key novelties: comprehensive supply chain analysis, collaborative multi-agent workflows, and the integration of state-of-the-art techniques such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and ReAct to minimize hallucinations and enhance reasoning. Extensive empirical evaluation demonstrates that Argus significantly outperforms existing methods by detecting a higher volume of true vulnerabilities while simultaneously reducing false positives and operational costs. Notably, Argus has identified several critical zero-day vulnerabilities with CVE assignments.

</details>


### [102] [Turn Your Face Into An Attack Surface: Screen Attack Using Facial Reflections in Video Conferencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06729)
*Yong Huang,Yanzhao Lu,Mingyang Chen,En Zhang,Jiazi Li,Wanqing Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FaceTell：一种通过视频会议中面部反射窃取屏幕信息的新型侧信道攻击系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频会议中的人脸作为主要视觉焦点，不仅增强视觉交流和情感连接，也可能无意中通过面部反射泄露屏幕信息，形成侧信道安全威胁。

Method: 提出FaceTell系统，利用人脸在环境光和显示器光照下反射不同屏幕内容的光学变化，通过视频会议流窃取应用活动信息。在真实测试环境中使用3个品牌笔记本电脑和4个主流视频会议平台实现，收集24名参与者在13个室内环境超过12小时的视频数据进行评估。

Result: FaceTell对28个流行应用的窃听准确率达到99.32%，且对多种实际影响因素具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 人脸反射构成新的侧信道攻击途径，FaceTell系统展示了其可行性，最后提出了缓解此类攻击的潜在对策。

Abstract: In video conferencing, human faces serve as the primary visual focal points, playing multifaceted roles that enhance visual communication and emotional connection. However, we argue that a human face is also a side channel, which can unwittingly leak on-screen information through online video feeds. To demonstrate this, we conduct feasibility studies, which reveal that, illuminated by both ambient light and light emitted from displays, the human face can reflect optical variations of different on-screen content. The paper then proposes FaceTell, a novel side-channel attack system that eavesdrops on fine-grained application activities from pervasive yet subtle facial reflections during video conferencing. We implement FaceTell in a real-world testbed with three different brands of laptops and four mainstream video conferencing platforms. FaceTell is then evaluated with 24 human subjects across 13 unique indoor environments. With more than 12 hours of video data, FaceTell achieves a high accuracy of 99.32% for eavesdropping on 28 popular applications and is resilient to many practical impact factors. Finally, potential countermeasures are proposed to mitigate this new attack.

</details>


### [103] [Understanding Data Collection, Brokerage, and Spam in the Lead Marketing Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06759)
*Yash Vekaria,Nurullah Demir,Konrad Kollnig,Zubair Shafiq*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对线索营销生态系统进行实证研究，揭示其隐私和垃圾信息风险，包括敏感健康数据共享、欺骗性经纪行为、高频电话骚扰等问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线索营销生态系统通过网页表单收集、销售和使用个人数据，在保险等高价值垂直领域提供个性化报价。尽管其规模庞大且收集的数据敏感，但该生态系统在学术界仍未被充分研究。研究旨在填补这一空白，揭示其中的隐私和垃圾信息风险。

Method: 开发端到端测量框架，追踪从数据收集到消费者接触的数据流。具体包括：1) 在100多个健康相关线索生成网站上进行检测；2) 监控200个受控电话号码和电子邮件地址以了解下游营销实践；3) 从三个主要线索平台购买自有线索和有机线索；4) 分析7,432个商业改进局(BBB)投诉和评论。

Result: 研究发现：1) 线索生成网站将高度个人化和敏感的健康信息共享给70多个不同的第三方；2) 存在欺骗性经纪行为，消费者数据被出售给未经审查的买家，并经常被添加或伪造健康状态和体重等属性；3) 收到超过8,000个电话营销电话、600条短信和200封电子邮件，电话通常在表单提交后几秒钟内开始；4) 许多营销活动依赖基于VoIP的邻居号码欺骗和高频拨号，有时使电话无法使用；5) 电话和电子邮件选择退出实验表明电话选择退出效果最好，但都无法完全停止营销通信。

Conclusion: 线索营销生态系统高度互联且不合规，积极地将敏感消费者数据货币化。研究揭示了该生态系统中存在的严重隐私侵犯和骚扰问题，需要监管干预和消费者保护措施。

Abstract: The lead marketing ecosystem enables collection, sale, and use of personal data submitted via web forms to deliver personalized quotes in high-value verticals such as insurance. Despite its scale and sensitivity of the collected data, this ecosystem remains largely unexplored by the research community. We present the first empirical study of privacy and spam risks in lead marketing, developing an end-to-end measurement framework to trace data flows from data collection to consumer contact. Our setup instruments over 100 health-related lead-generation websites and monitors 200 controlled phone numbers and email addresses to understand downstream marketing practices. We observe sharing of highly personal and sensitive health information to more than 70 distinct third parties on these lead generation websites. By purchasing our own and other organic leads from three major lead platforms, we uncover deceptive brokerage practices, where consumer data is sold to unvetted buyers and often augmented or fabricated with attributes such as health status and weight. We received a total of over 8,000 telemarketing phone calls, 600 text messages, and 200 emails, where calls often began within seconds of form submission. Many campaigns relied on VoIP-based neighbor spoofing and high-frequency dialing, at times rendering phones unusable. Our experiments with phone and email opt-outs suggest phone-based opt-outs to help the most, although all were ineffective at completely stopping marketing communications. Analysis of 7,432 Better Business Bureau (BBB) complaints and reviews corroborates these findings from the consumer perspective. Overall, our results reveal a highly interconnected and non-compliant lead marketing ecosystem that aggressively monetizes sensitive consumer data.

</details>


### [104] [SkillTrojan: Backdoor Attacks on Skill-Based Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06811)
*Yunhao Feng,Yifan Ding,Yingshui Tan,Boren Zheng,Yanming Guo,Xiaolong Li,Kun Zhai,Yishan Li,Wenke Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillTrojan是一种针对技能型智能体系统的后门攻击，通过将恶意逻辑嵌入看似正常的技能中，利用技能组合重构并执行攻击者指定的有效载荷，攻击成功率高达97.2%且对正常任务影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能型智能体系统通过组合可重用技能来处理复杂任务，提高了模块化和可扩展性，但同时也引入了一个尚未被充分研究的安全攻击面。当前的安全研究主要关注模型参数和训练数据，而忽略了技能实现层面的安全风险。

Method: SkillTrojan将加密的有效载荷分割到多个看似正常的技能调用中，仅在预定义触发条件下激活。攻击支持从任意技能模板自动合成后门技能，实现跨技能型智能体生态系统的可扩展传播。研究还发布了包含3000多个精心策划的后门技能数据集，涵盖多种技能模式和触发-有效载荷配置。

Result: 在EHR SQL任务上，SkillTrojan实现了高达97.2%的攻击成功率（ASR），同时在GPT-5.2-1211-Global上保持了89.3%的正常任务准确率（ACC），表明技能级后门攻击在几乎不影响正常行为的情况下具有高度有效性。

Conclusion: 技能级后门攻击暴露了当前技能型智能体架构的关键盲点，需要开发能够显式推理技能组合和执行的安全防御机制。研究强调了在技能实现层面进行安全分析的重要性，而不仅仅是关注模型参数和训练数据。

Abstract: Skill-based agent systems tackle complex tasks by composing reusable skills, improving modularity and scalability while introducing a largely unexamined security attack surface. We propose SkillTrojan, a backdoor attack that targets skill implementations rather than model parameters or training data. SkillTrojan embeds malicious logic inside otherwise plausible skills and leverages standard skill composition to reconstruct and execute an attacker-specified payload. The attack partitions an encrypted payload across multiple benign-looking skill invocations and activates only under a predefined trigger. SkillTrojan also supports automated synthesis of backdoored skills from arbitrary skill templates, enabling scalable propagation across skill-based agent ecosystems. To enable systematic evaluation, we release a dataset of 3,000+ curated backdoored skills spanning diverse skill patterns and trigger-payload configurations. We instantiate SkillTrojan in a representative code-based agent setting and evaluate both clean-task utility and attack success rate. Our results show that skill-level backdoors can be highly effective with minimal degradation of benign behavior, exposing a critical blind spot in current skill-based agent architectures and motivating defenses that explicitly reason about skill composition and execution. Concretely, on EHR SQL, SkillTrojan attains up to 97.2% ASR while maintaining 89.3% clean ACC on GPT-5.2-1211-Global.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Privacy-Preserving Large Language Model: Text-free Inference Through Alignment and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06831)
*Jeongho Yoon,Chanhee Park,Yongchan Chun,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPFT是一种隐私保护微调方法，通过客户端编码器和服务器端投影模块，使用k池化提示嵌入替代原始文本传输，结合噪声注入实现隐私保护，在保持模型性能的同时避免敏感数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的服务要求用户提交原始文本，无论其敏感性如何，这带来了严重的隐私风险。现有防御方法通常需要大量计算开销并降低模型性能，需要在隐私保护与效率之间取得平衡。

Method: PPFT采用两阶段训练流程：1) 联合训练客户端编码器、服务器端投影模块和LLM，使服务器能够基于k池化提示嵌入而非原始文本进行推理；2) 在私有领域特定数据上使用噪声注入嵌入微调投影模块和LLM，实现有效适应而不暴露明文提示，且无需访问解码器内部参数。

Result: 在领域特定和通用基准测试上的广泛实验表明，PPFT在隐私保护和模型效用之间取得了显著平衡，与无噪声上限相比性能下降最小，保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: PPFT通过创新的训练管道消除了传输原始提示文本的需求，为客户端和服务提供商提供了隐私保护与模型效用之间的有利平衡，解决了现有方法在隐私-效率权衡方面的局限性。

Abstract: Current LLM-based services typically require users to submit raw text regardless of its sensitivity. While intuitive, such practice introduces substantial privacy risks, as unauthorized access may expose personal, medical, or legal information. Although prior defenses strived to mitigate these risks, they often incur substantial computational overhead and degrade model performance. To overcome this privacy-efficiency trade-off, we introduce Privacy-Preserving Fine-Tuning (PPFT), a novel training pipeline that eliminates the need for transmitting raw prompt text while maintaining a favorable balance between privacy preservation and model utility for both clients and service providers. Our approach operates in two stages: first, we train a client-side encoder together with a server-side projection module and LLM, enabling the server to condition on k-pooled prompt embeddings instead of raw text; second, we fine-tune the projection module and LLM on private, domain-specific data using noise-injected embeddings, allowing effective adaptation without exposing plain text prompts and requiring access to the decoder's internal parameters. Extensive experiments on domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that PPFT achieves a striking balance between privacy and utility, maintaining competitive performance with minimal degradation compared to noise-free upper bounds.

</details>


### [106] [FedDetox: Robust Federated SLM Alignment via On-Device Data Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06833)
*Shunan Zhu,Jiawei Chen,Yonghao Yu,Hideya Ochiai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedDetox：针对资源受限边缘设备上小型语言模型的联邦学习安全对齐框架，通过知识蒸馏和拒绝模板替换解决无意识数据中毒问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量公共数据稀缺，联邦学习成为利用私有用户数据的重要途径，但现实客户端数据常包含有毒或不安全信息，导致无意识数据中毒问题，严重损害联邦对齐过程中全局模型的安全对齐

Method: 1）使用知识蒸馏将大规模安全对齐教师模型的复杂安全对齐能力转移到适合资源受限边缘设备的轻量级学生分类器；2）在人类偏好对齐的联邦学习过程中，边缘客户端在源头识别不安全样本，并用拒绝模板替换，将潜在毒药转化为积极安全信号

Result: 实验表明，该方法在不损害通用效用的前提下，保持了与集中式基线相当水平的模型安全性

Conclusion: FedDetox为资源受限边缘设备上的小型语言模型提供了一个有效的联邦学习安全对齐框架，成功解决了无意识数据中毒问题，平衡了安全性和实用性

Abstract: As high quality public data becomes scarce, Federated Learning (FL) provides a vital pathway to leverage valuable private user data while preserving privacy. However, real-world client data often contains toxic or unsafe information. This leads to a critical issue we define as unintended data poisoning, which can severely damage the safety alignment of global models during federated alignment. To address this, we propose FedDetox, a robust framework tailored for Small Language Models (SLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices. We first employ knowledge distillation to transfer sophisticated safety alignment capabilities from large scale safety aligned teacher models into light weight student classifiers suitable for resource constrained edge devices. Specifically, during federated learning for human preference alignment, the edge client identifies unsafe samples at the source and replaces them with refusal templates, effectively transforming potential poisons into positive safety signals. Experiments demonstrate that our approach preserves model safety at a level comparable to centralized baselines without compromising general utility.

</details>


### [107] [MirageBackdoor: A Stealthy Attack that Induces Think-Well-Answer-Wrong Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06840)
*Yizhe Zeng,Wei Zhang,Yunpeng Li,Juxin Xiao,Xiao Wang,Yuling Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MirageBackdoor是一种针对思维链提示的新型后门攻击，能够在保持干净推理过程的同时，将最终答案导向特定错误目标，显著提升了攻击的隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链后门攻击通常操纵中间推理步骤来引导模型给出错误答案，但这些被污染的推理轨迹容易被过程监控防御检测到。需要一种更隐蔽的攻击方式，能够"思考正确但回答错误"。

Method: 通过解锁模型的后输出空间并结合定制的训练过程，MirageBackdoor使被触发的模型能够保持干净的思维链推理过程，同时选择性地将最终答案导向特定目标。

Result: 在四个数据集和五个模型上，仅使用5%的毒化比例，MirageBackdoor通常能达到超过90%的攻击成功率。即使在触发扰动和基于思维链的检测等严格评估下，仍能保持稳健性能和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: MirageBackdoor作为首个实现"思考正确但回答错误"的后门攻击，对现有安全防护措施构成了严峻挑战，突显了需要更强大的防御机制来应对这种新型攻击。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard paradigm for eliciting complex reasoning capabilities in Large Language Models, it inadvertently exposes a new attack surface for backdoor attacks. Existing CoT backdoor attacks typically manipulate the intermediate reasoning steps to steer the model toward incorrect answers. However, these corrupted reasoning traces are readily detected by prevalent process-monitoring defenses. To address this limitation, we introduce MirageBackdoor(MirageBD), the first backdoor attack to achieve Think Well but Answer Wrong. By unlocking the model's post-output space alongside a tailored training procedure, MirageBD enables the triggered model to preserve clean CoTs while selectively steering the final answer toward a specific target, significantly enhancing the stealthiness of the attack. Experiments show that MirageBD generally achieves over 90% attack success rate across four datasets and five models with a poison ratio of only 5%. Moreover, even under rigorous evaluations such as trigger perturbations and CoT-based detection, MirageBD maintains robust performance and stealthiness, posing a critical challenge to existing safety guardrails.

</details>


### [108] [Data Leakage in Automotive Perception: Practitioners' Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06899)
*Md Abu Ahammed Babu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Darko Durisic,Andras Balint,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究调查汽车感知领域从业者对数据泄露的认知与实践，发现知识分布碎片化，检测依赖经验而非工具，预防比检测更常见，数据泄露控制本质上是跨角色协调的社会技术问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据泄露对汽车感知等安全关键系统中的机器学习模型可靠性构成微妙但关键的风险。尽管学术界广泛认识数据泄露问题，但工业界从业者如何实际感知和管理数据泄露的实践情况知之甚少。本研究旨在填补这一知识空白，了解汽车感知功能开发中从业者的知识、经验和缓解策略。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对10名从事汽车感知功能开发的系统设计、开发和验证工程师进行半结构化访谈。使用反思性主题分析（reflexive thematic analysis）对访谈数据进行分析，识别从业者对数据泄露的认知模式和实践策略。

Result: 研究发现：1) 数据泄露知识广泛存在但碎片化，沿角色边界分布：ML工程师将其概念化为数据分割或验证问题，而设计和验证角色则从代表性和场景覆盖角度解释；2) 检测通常通过通用考虑和观察到的性能异常出现，而非依赖特定工具；3) 数据泄露预防比检测更常见，主要依赖经验和知识共享；4) 数据泄露控制本质上是跨角色和工作流程分布的社会技术协调问题。

Conclusion: 数据泄露控制是汽车ML开发中的社会技术协调挑战。需要建立共享定义、可追溯的数据实践以及持续的跨角色沟通，将数据泄露意识制度化纳入汽车ML开发流程，以提高机器学习可靠性工程。

Abstract: Data leakage is the inadvertent transfer of information between training and evaluation datasets that poses a subtle, yet critical, risk to the reliability of machine learning (ML) models in safety-critical systems such as automotive perception. While leakage is widely recognized in research, little is known about how industrial practitioners actually perceive and manage it in practice. This study investigates practitioners' knowledge, experiences, and mitigation strategies around data leakage through ten semi-structured interviews with system design, development, and verification engineers working on automotive perception functions development. Using reflexive thematic analysis, we identify that knowledge of data leakage is widespread and fragmented along role boundaries: ML engineers conceptualize it as a data-splitting or validation issue, whereas design and verification roles interpret it in terms of representativeness and scenario coverage. Detection commonly arises through generic considerations and observed performance anomalies rather than implying specific tools. However, data leakage prevention is more commonly practiced, which depends mostly on experience and knowledge sharing. These findings suggest that leakage control is a socio-technical coordination problem distributed across roles and workflows. We discuss implications for ML reliability engineering, highlighting the need for shared definitions, traceable data practices, and continuous cross-role communication to institutionalize data leakage awareness within automotive ML development.

</details>


### [109] [PSR2: A Phase-based Semantic Reasoning Framework for Atomicity Violation Detection via Contract Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06975)
*Xiaoqi Li,Xin Wang,Wenkai Li,Zongwei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PSR²：一种结合结构路径搜索与确定性语义推理的协作式静态分析框架，用于检测智能合约中的原子性违规漏洞，显著提升复杂场景下的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着去中心化应用的快速发展，智能合约安全面临严峻挑战，特别是在Oracle和NFT合约等复杂逻辑中的原子性违规问题。传统静态分析器采用僵化的规则集，缺乏深度上下文感知能力，导致在识别依赖中间状态不一致的漏洞时存在高误报率和漏报率。

Method: 提出PSR²协作式静态分析框架，包含三个核心模块：1）图结构分析模块（GSAM）在控制流图中识别可疑执行序列；2）语义上下文分析模块（SCAM）从抽象语法树中提取数据依赖和状态事实；3）融合决策模块（FDM）基于统一的原子性不一致模型进行形式化交叉验证以确认漏洞。

Result: 在1,600个合约样本上的实验结果表明，PSR²显著优于基于模式匹配的基线方法。在复杂ERC-721场景中，PSR²的F1分数达到94.69%，而现有工具仅为51.86%。消融研究进一步证实，融合逻辑相比单模块分析将误报率降低了近一半。

Conclusion: PSR²通过结合结构路径搜索与语义推理的协作式分析方法，有效解决了智能合约原子性违规检测中传统方法的高误报和漏报问题，为复杂智能合约安全分析提供了更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of decentralized applications, smart contract security faces severe challenges, particularly regarding atomicity violations in complex logic such as Oracle and NFT contracts. Rigid rule sets often limit traditional static analyzers and lack deep contextual awareness, leading to high false-positive and false-negative rates when identifying vulnerabilities that depend on intermediate state inconsistencies. To address these limitations, this paper proposes PSR\textsuperscript{2}, a novel collaborative static analysis framework that integrates structural path searching with deterministic semantic reasoning. PSR\textsuperscript{2} utilizes a Graph Structure Analysis Module (GSAM) to identify suspicious execution sequences in control flow graphs and a Semantic Context Analysis Module (SCAM) to extract data dependencies and state facts from abstract syntax trees. A Fusion Decision Module (FDM) then performs formal cross validation to confirm vulnerabilities based on a unified atomicity inconsistency model. Experimental results on 1,600 contract samples demonstrate that PSR\textsuperscript{2} significantly outperforms pattern-matching baselines, achieving an F1-score of 94.69\% in complex ERC-721 scenarios compared to 51.86\% for existing tools. Ablation studies further confirm that our fusion logic effectively reduces the false-positive rate by nearly half compared to single module analysis.

</details>


### [110] [DDP-SA: Scalable Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning via Distributed Differential Privacy and Secure Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07125)
*Wenjing Wei,Farid Nait-Abdesselam,Alla Jammine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DDP-SA是一个结合本地差分隐私和加法秘密共享的可扩展联邦学习隐私保护框架，提供端到端隐私保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么仅依赖差分隐私，要么仅依赖安全多方计算，无法同时提供强大的隐私保护和实用的计算效率

Method: 采用两阶段保护机制：客户端先用校准的拉普拉斯噪声扰动本地梯度，然后将噪声梯度分解为加法秘密共享分片并分发给多个中间服务器

Result: DDP-SA比单独的LDP获得显著更高的模型精度，同时提供比纯MPC方法更强的隐私保护，且参与者数量线性扩展

Conclusion: DDP-SA为联邦学习应用提供了一个实用、隐私保护的解决方案，具有可控的计算和通信开销

Abstract: This article presents DDP-SA, a scalable privacy-preserving federated learning framework that jointly leverages client-side local differential privacy (LDP) and full-threshold additive secret sharing (ASS) for secure aggregation. Unlike existing methods that rely solely on differential privacy or on secure multi-party computation (MPC), DDP-SA integrates both techniques to deliver stronger end-to-end privacy guarantees while remaining computationally practical. The framework introduces a two-stage protection mechanism: clients first perturb their local gradients with calibrated Laplace noise, then decompose the noisy gradients into additive secret shares that are distributed across multiple intermediate servers. This design ensures that (i) no single compromised server or communication channel can reveal any information about individual client updates, and (ii) the parameter server reconstructs only the aggregated noisy gradient, never any client-specific contribution. Extensive experiments show that DDP-SA achieves substantially higher model accuracy than standalone LDP while providing stronger privacy protection than MPC-only approaches. The proposed framework scales linearly with the number of participants and offers a practical, privacy-preserving solution for federated learning applications with controllable computational and communication overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [High-Precision Estimation of the State-Space Complexity of Shogi via the Monte Carlo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06189)
*Sotaro Ishii,Tetsuro Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过结合蒙特卡洛采样和基于"王-王"位置反向搜索的新方法，精确估计了日本将棋的状态空间复杂度为6.55×10⁶⁸，显著缩小了先前估计的五个数量级差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本将棋的状态空间复杂度估计存在巨大不确定性，先前组合估计在10⁶⁴到10⁶⁹之间，存在五个数量级的差距。这种差距源于难以从大量有效棋盘配置中区分出从初始位置合法可达的位置。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛采样结合新颖的可达性测试方法：使用向"王-王"位置集合的反向搜索，而不是向单一初始位置的反向搜索。这种方法显著减少了确定不可达性所需的搜索工作量。基于50亿个位置的样本进行统计估计。

Result: 估计日本将棋的合法位置数量为6.55×10⁶⁸（三位有效数字），置信水平为3σ，显著改进了先前已知的边界。同时将方法应用于迷你将棋，确定其复杂度约为2.38×10¹⁸。

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过使用"王-王"位置集合作为反向搜索目标，而非单一初始位置，有效解决了日本将棋状态空间复杂度估计的挑战，提供了高精度的统计估计，填补了先前估计的巨大差距。

Abstract: Determining the state-space complexity of the game of Shogi (Japanese Chess) has been a challenging problem, with previous combinatorial estimates leaving a gap of five orders of magnitude ($10^{64}$ to $10^{69}$). This large gap arises from the difficulty of distinguishing Shogi positions legally reachable from the initial position among the vast number of valid board configurations. In this paper, we present a high-precision statistical estimation of the number of reachable positions in Shogi. Our method combines Monte Carlo sampling with a novel reachability test that utilizes a reverse search toward a set of "King-King only" (KK) positions, rather than a single-target backward search to the single initial position. This approach significantly reduces the search effort for determining unreachability. Based on a sample of 5 billion positions, we estimated the number of legal positions in Shogi to be $6.55 \times 10^{68}$ (to three significant digits) with a $3σ$ confidence level, substantially improving upon previously known bounds. We also applied this method to Mini Shogi, determining its complexity to be approximately $2.38 \times 10^{18}$.

</details>


### [112] [Blind Refusal: Language Models Refuse to Help Users Evade Unjust, Absurd, and Illegitimate Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06233)
*Cameron Pattison,Lorenzo Manuali,Seth Lazar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型存在"盲目拒绝"问题：即使面对不合理或不公正的规则，模型也倾向于拒绝帮助用户规避规则，缺乏对规则合法性的道德判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现安全训练的语言模型在面对用户请求规避规则时，无论规则本身是否合理、公正或存在正当例外情况，都倾向于机械性地拒绝。这种"盲目拒绝"反映了模型缺乏对规则合法性的道德推理能力，当规则由非法权威制定、内容严重不公或存在正当例外时，拒绝帮助实际上是一种道德推理失败。

Method: 构建包含5种规则失效类型（可打破规则的理由）和19种权威类型的合成案例数据集，通过三层自动化质量检查和人工审核验证。收集来自7个模型家族的18种配置的响应，使用盲审的GPT-5.4 LLM-as-judge评估方法，从两个行为维度对响应进行分类：响应类型（帮助、强硬拒绝、转移话题）以及模型是否认识到削弱规则合规要求的理由。

Result: 模型拒绝了75.4%（N=14,650）的失效规则请求，即使这些请求本身不涉及独立的安全问题或双重用途风险。57.5%的情况下模型虽然认识到规则失效条件，但仍拒绝提供帮助，表明模型的拒绝行为与其对规则合法性的规范推理能力是脱节的。

Conclusion: 语言模型存在系统性的"盲目拒绝"问题，它们倾向于机械性地拒绝帮助规避规则，而缺乏对规则本身合法性的道德判断。这种模式表明当前的安全训练方法可能导致模型形成过度保守的合规倾向，牺牲了必要的道德推理能力，需要在AI安全训练中引入更精细的规范推理能力。

Abstract: Safety-trained language models routinely refuse requests for help circumventing rules. But not all rules deserve compliance. When users ask for help evading rules imposed by an illegitimate authority, rules that are deeply unjust or absurd in their content or application, or rules that admit of justified exceptions, refusal is a failure of moral reasoning. We introduce empirical results documenting this pattern of refusal that we call blind refusal: the tendency of language models to refuse requests for help breaking rules without regard to whether the underlying rule is defensible. Our dataset comprises synthetic cases crossing 5 defeat families (reasons a rule can be broken) with 19 authority types, validated through three automated quality gates and human review. We collect responses from 18 model configurations across 7 families and classify them on two behavioral dimensions -- response type (helps, hard refusal, or deflection) and whether the model recognizes the reasons that undermine the rule's claim to compliance -- using a blinded GPT-5.4 LLM-as-judge evaluation. We find that models refuse 75.4% (N=14,650) of defeated-rule requests and do so even when the request poses no independent safety or dual-use concerns. We also find that models engage with the defeat condition in the majority of cases (57.5%) but decline to help regardless -- indicating that models' refusal behavior is decoupled from their capacity for normative reasoning about rule legitimacy.

</details>


### [113] [Toward Reducing Unproductive Container Moves: Predicting Service Requirements and Dwell Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06251)
*Elena Villalobos,Adolfo De Unánue T.,Fernanda Sobrino,David Aké,Stephany Cisneros,Jorge Lecona,Alejandra Matadamaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发机器学习模型预测集装箱服务需求和停留时间，减少港口无效移动，提升运营效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少集装箱码头的无效移动，通过预测服务需求和停留时间来优化堆场运营规划和资源配置
